- **bond-challenge**: Fetch the wallet-ownership challenge for a provider bond (#1967). ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-challenge.md))
- **bond-create**: Create or top-up a provider's performance bond. Non-admin callers must prove control of the provider's registered wallet: pass --wallet/--private-key/--private-key-file and the CLI fetches a challenge and signs it automatically (#1967). Without a key source the request goes unsigned — admin JWTs still pass, provider callers get 403. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-create.md))
- **bond-lock**: Lock a provider's bond while a high-value job is in flight. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-lock.md))
- **bond-lock-onchain**: Submit the on-chain BOND_LOCK escrow transaction for a provider bond. ``bond create``/``top-up`` record the bond coordinator-side but require a prior chain-verified ``BOND_LOCK``; this command is that missing leg. The tx pays ``--escrow-address`` (the node's ``BOND_ESCROW_ADDRESS``) with payload ``{bond_id, provider, lock_days}`` where ``provider`` is the signing wallet's own address — the chain r ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-lock-onchain.md))
- **bond-release**: Release a previously locked provider bond. The coordinator's release endpoint un-locks a LOCKED bond back to ACTIVE: the bond record and its amount stay posted — release is not a withdrawal, and there is no endpoint that frees the bonded funds. ``status: "active"`` in the response therefore *is* the released state, not a failure. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-release.md))
